# Per-note context searches

Generated: 2026-11-17 18:23:23 UTC

All searches occurred after the transcription freeze. The corpus is limited to the supplied context text files and text extracted from the supplied Oda paper; no scan OCR or other Library material is used.

Method: ripgrep, case-insensitive fixed-string OR matching, one search for each substantive note and each standing-question row. Counts are matching lines, not mathematical occurrences; all matching locators are retained in context_note_searches.json. This Markdown report gives up to six locators per file, preferring the relevant Hua section.

The substantive note itself supplies the file/scan comparison and whether the current pages add, restate, differ or leave no close match. A zero hit is not proof that an equivalent formulation is absent.
